  \Gscale@div\@tempb{\linewidth}{\wd\pandoc@box}%

  \ifdim\@tempa\p@<\p@\scalebox{\@tempa}{\usebox\pandoc@box}%
  \else\usebox{\pandoc@box}%
  \fi%
}

\subsubsection{standardise column names and creating condition
variables}\label{standardise-column-names-and-creating-condition-variables}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\NormalTok{df }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{clean\_names}\NormalTok{(df)}

\NormalTok{df }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ df }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{condition =} \FunctionTok{case\_when}\NormalTok{(}
    \SpecialCharTok{!}\FunctionTok{is.na}\NormalTok{(mpq7) }\SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \StringTok{"MP"}\NormalTok{,}
    \SpecialCharTok{!}\FunctionTok{is.na}\NormalTok{(emq7) }\SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \StringTok{"EM"}\NormalTok{,}
    \SpecialCharTok{!}\FunctionTok{is.na}\NormalTok{(arq7) }\SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \StringTok{"AR"}\NormalTok{,}
    \ConstantTok{TRUE} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \ConstantTok{NA\_character\_}
\NormalTok{  ))}

\FunctionTok{table}\NormalTok{(df}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{condition)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{verbatim}
## 
## AR EM MP 
## 70 69 71
\end{verbatim}

\subsubsection{Coalescing to create a single shared column to compare
across
conditions}\label{coalescing-to-create-a-single-shared-column-to-compare-across-conditions}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\NormalTok{df }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ df }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}
    \AttributeTok{ai\_use\_1 =} \FunctionTok{coalesce}\NormalTok{(mpq2\_1, emq2\_1, arq2\_1),}
    \AttributeTok{ai\_use\_2 =} \FunctionTok{coalesce}\NormalTok{(mpq2\_2, emq2\_2, arq2\_2),}
    \AttributeTok{ai\_use\_3 =} \FunctionTok{coalesce}\NormalTok{(mpq2\_3, emq2\_3, arq2\_3),}
    \AttributeTok{ai\_use\_4 =} \FunctionTok{coalesce}\NormalTok{(mpq2\_4, emq2\_4, arq2\_4),}
    \AttributeTok{ai\_use\_5 =} \FunctionTok{coalesce}\NormalTok{(mpq2\_5, emq2\_5, arq2\_5),}
    \AttributeTok{ai\_use\_6 =} \FunctionTok{coalesce}\NormalTok{(mpq2\_6, emq2\_6, arq2\_6),}
    \AttributeTok{ai\_disclosure\_1 =} \FunctionTok{coalesce}\NormalTok{(mpq3\_1, emq3\_1, arq3\_1),}
    \AttributeTok{ai\_disclosure\_2 =} \FunctionTok{coalesce}\NormalTok{(mpq3\_2, emq3\_2, arq3\_2),}
    \AttributeTok{ai\_disclosure\_3 =} \FunctionTok{coalesce}\NormalTok{(mpq3\_3, emq3\_3, arq3\_3),}
    \AttributeTok{ai\_disclosure\_4 =} \FunctionTok{coalesce}\NormalTok{(mpq3\_4, emq3\_4, arq3\_4),}
    \AttributeTok{ai\_disclosure\_5 =} \FunctionTok{coalesce}\NormalTok{(mpq3\_5, emq3\_5, arq3\_5),}
    \AttributeTok{ai\_disclosure\_6 =} \FunctionTok{coalesce}\NormalTok{(mpq3\_6, emq3\_6, arq3\_6),}
    \AttributeTok{mancheck1\_1 =} \FunctionTok{coalesce}\NormalTok{(mpq4\_1, emq4\_1, arq4\_1),}
    \AttributeTok{mancheck1\_2 =} \FunctionTok{coalesce}\NormalTok{(mpq4\_2, emq4\_2, arq4\_2),}
    \AttributeTok{mancheck1\_3 =} \FunctionTok{coalesce}\NormalTok{(mpq4\_3, emq4\_3, arq4\_3),}
    \AttributeTok{mancheck2\_1 =} \FunctionTok{coalesce}\NormalTok{(mpq5\_1, emq5\_1, arq5\_1),}
    \AttributeTok{mancheck2\_2 =} \FunctionTok{coalesce}\NormalTok{(mpq5\_2, emq5\_2, arq5\_2),}
    \AttributeTok{mancheck2\_3 =} \FunctionTok{coalesce}\NormalTok{(mpq5\_3, emq5\_3, arq5\_3)}
\NormalTok{  )}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsubsection{Recoding AI diclosure (scale of 1-6 to 1-5) by collapting
1 and 2 (I do not use AI and Most
Unlikely)}\label{recoding-ai-diclosure-scale-of-1-6-to-1-5-by-collapting-1-and-2-i-do-not-use-ai-and-most-unlikely}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\NormalTok{recode\_disclosure }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \ControlFlowTok{function}\NormalTok{(x) \{}
  \FunctionTok{case\_when}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{    x }\SpecialCharTok{==} \DecValTok{1} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{,}
\NormalTok{    x }\SpecialCharTok{==} \DecValTok{2} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{,}
\NormalTok{    x }\SpecialCharTok{==} \DecValTok{3} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{,}
\NormalTok{    x }\SpecialCharTok{==} \DecValTok{4} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \DecValTok{3}\NormalTok{,}
\NormalTok{    x }\SpecialCharTok{==} \DecValTok{5} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \DecValTok{4}\NormalTok{,}
\NormalTok{    x }\SpecialCharTok{==} \DecValTok{6} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \DecValTok{5}\NormalTok{,}
    \ConstantTok{TRUE} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \ConstantTok{NA\_real\_}
\NormalTok{  )}
\NormalTok{\}}

\NormalTok{df }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ df }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{across}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{starts\_with}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"ai\_disclosure"}\NormalTok{), recode\_disclosure))}

\CommentTok{\# NA check {-} should all be 0}
\NormalTok{df }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{select}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{starts\_with}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"ai\_use"}\NormalTok{), }\FunctionTok{starts\_with}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"ai\_disclosure"}\NormalTok{),}
         \FunctionTok{starts\_with}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"mancheck"}\NormalTok{)) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{summarise}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{across}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{everything}\NormalTok{(), }\SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}}\FunctionTok{sum}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{is.na}\NormalTok{(.))))}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{verbatim}
## # A tibble: 1 x 18
##   ai_use_1 ai_use_2 ai_use_3 ai_use_4 ai_use_5 ai_use_6 ai_disclosure_1
##      <int>    <int>    <int>    <int>    <int>    <int>           <int>
## 1        0        0        0        0        0        0               0
## # i 11 more variables: ai_disclosure_2 <int>, ai_disclosure_3 <int>,
## #   ai_disclosure_4 <int>, ai_disclosure_5 <int>, ai_disclosure_6 <int>,
## #   mancheck1_1 <int>, mancheck1_2 <int>, mancheck1_3 <int>, mancheck2_1 <int>,
## #   mancheck2_2 <int>, mancheck2_3 <int>
\end{verbatim}

\subsubsection{Checking Missing Data (across all
columns)}\label{checking-missing-data-across-all-columns}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\NormalTok{missing\_summary }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ df }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{summarise}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{across}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{everything}\NormalTok{(), }\SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}}\FunctionTok{sum}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{is.na}\NormalTok{(.)))) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{pivot\_longer}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{everything}\NormalTok{(), }\AttributeTok{names\_to =} \StringTok{"variable"}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{values\_to =} \StringTok{"n\_missing"}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{filter}\NormalTok{(n\_missing }\SpecialCharTok{\textgreater{}} \DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{percent\_missing =} \FunctionTok{round}\NormalTok{(n\_missing }\SpecialCharTok{/} \FunctionTok{nrow}\NormalTok{(df) }\SpecialCharTok{*} \DecValTok{100}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{)) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{arrange}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{desc}\NormalTok{(n\_missing))}

\FunctionTok{print}\NormalTok{(missing\_summary)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsubsection{Comprehension Check}\label{comprehension-check}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\NormalTok{df }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ df }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}
    \AttributeTok{comprehension\_fail =} \FunctionTok{case\_when}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{      condition }\SpecialCharTok{==} \StringTok{"MP"} \SpecialCharTok{\&}\NormalTok{ mpq7 }\SpecialCharTok{!=} \DecValTok{2} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{,}
\NormalTok{      condition }\SpecialCharTok{==} \StringTok{"EM"} \SpecialCharTok{\&}\NormalTok{ emq7 }\SpecialCharTok{!=} \DecValTok{2} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{,}
\NormalTok{      condition }\SpecialCharTok{==} \StringTok{"AR"} \SpecialCharTok{\&}\NormalTok{ arq7 }\SpecialCharTok{!=} \DecValTok{2} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{,}
      \ConstantTok{TRUE} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \ConstantTok{FALSE}
\NormalTok{    )}
\NormalTok{  )}

\CommentTok{\# how many failed by condition}
\NormalTok{df }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{group\_by}\NormalTok{(condition) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{summarise}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{n\_failed =} \FunctionTok{sum}\NormalTok{(comprehension\_fail))}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{verbatim}
## # A tibble: 3 x 2
##   condition n_failed
##   <chr>        <int>
## 1 AR               3
## 2 EM               2
## 3 MP               4
\end{verbatim}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\#primary sample excluding comprehension failures}
\NormalTok{df\_primary }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ df }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{filter}\NormalTok{(comprehension\_fail }\SpecialCharTok{==} \ConstantTok{FALSE}\NormalTok{)}

\CommentTok{\#sample size and condition counts}
\FunctionTok{cat}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"Primary sample size:"}\NormalTok{, }\FunctionTok{nrow}\NormalTok{(df\_primary), }\StringTok{"}\SpecialCharTok{\textbackslash{}n}\StringTok{"}\NormalTok{)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{verbatim}

\end{verbatim}

\begin{Highlighting}[]
\FunctionTok{table}\NormalTok{(df\_primary}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{condition)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsubsection{Comprehension check and exclusion - n = 201, 9 fails
(excluded)}\label{comprehension-check-and-exclusion---n-201-9-fails-excluded}

\subsection{Who failed comprehension
check?}\label{who-failed-comprehension-check}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\#comprehension failures are disproportionately non{-}native English speakers?}
\NormalTok{df }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{native\_english =} \FunctionTok{ifelse}\NormalTok{(b2q4 }\SpecialCharTok{==} \DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"Native"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"Non{-}native"}\NormalTok{)) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{group\_by}\NormalTok{(comprehension\_fail, native\_english) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{summarise}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{n =} \FunctionTok{n}\NormalTok{()) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{pivot\_wider}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{names\_from =}\NormalTok{ native\_english, }\AttributeTok{values\_from =}\NormalTok{ n, }\AttributeTok{values\_fill =} \DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{total =}\NormalTok{ Native }\SpecialCharTok{+} \StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\AttributeTok{Non{-}native}\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\NormalTok{,}
         \AttributeTok{pct\_non\_native =} \FunctionTok{round}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\AttributeTok{Non{-}native}\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}} \SpecialCharTok{/}\NormalTok{ total }\SpecialCharTok{*} \DecValTok{100}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{))}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{verbatim}
## `summarise()` has regrouped the output.
## i Summaries were computed grouped by comprehension_fail and native_english.
## i Output is grouped by comprehension_fail.
## i Use `summarise(.groups = "drop_last")` to silence this message.
## i Use `summarise(.by = c(comprehension_fail, native_english))` for
##   per-operation grouping (`?dplyr::dplyr_by`) instead.
\end{verbatim}

\begin{verbatim}

\section{RESULT: 8 out of 9 are non native English
speakers}\label{result-8-out-of-9-are-non-native-english-speakers}

\subsubsection{Inconsistency check}\label{inconsistency-check}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Flag items where ai\_use = 1 (Highly Unlikely) but ai\_disclosure \textgreater{}= 3}
\NormalTok{df\_primary }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ df\_primary }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}
    \AttributeTok{inconsistent\_1 =}\NormalTok{ ai\_use\_1 }\SpecialCharTok{==} \DecValTok{1} \SpecialCharTok{\&}\NormalTok{ ai\_disclosure\_1 }\SpecialCharTok{\textgreater{}=} \DecValTok{3}\NormalTok{,}
    \AttributeTok{inconsistent\_2 =}\NormalTok{ ai\_use\_2 }\SpecialCharTok{==} \DecValTok{1} \SpecialCharTok{\&}\NormalTok{ ai\_disclosure\_2 }\SpecialCharTok{\textgreater{}=} \DecValTok{3}\NormalTok{,}
    \AttributeTok{inconsistent\_3 =}\NormalTok{ ai\_use\_3 }\SpecialCharTok{==} \DecValTok{1} \SpecialCharTok{\&}\NormalTok{ ai\_disclosure\_3 }\SpecialCharTok{\textgreater{}=} \DecValTok{3}\NormalTok{,}
    \AttributeTok{inconsistent\_4 =}\NormalTok{ ai\_use\_4 }\SpecialCharTok{==} \DecValTok{1} \SpecialCharTok{\&}\NormalTok{ ai\_disclosure\_4 }\SpecialCharTok{\textgreater{}=} \DecValTok{3}\NormalTok{,}
    \AttributeTok{inconsistent\_5 =}\NormalTok{ ai\_use\_5 }\SpecialCharTok{==} \DecValTok{1} \SpecialCharTok{\&}\NormalTok{ ai\_disclosure\_5 }\SpecialCharTok{\textgreater{}=} \DecValTok{3}\NormalTok{,}
    \AttributeTok{inconsistent\_6 =}\NormalTok{ ai\_use\_6 }\SpecialCharTok{==} \DecValTok{1} \SpecialCharTok{\&}\NormalTok{ ai\_disclosure\_6 }\SpecialCharTok{\textgreater{}=} \DecValTok{3}\NormalTok{,}
    \AttributeTok{n\_inconsistent =}\NormalTok{ inconsistent\_1 }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ inconsistent\_2 }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ inconsistent\_3 }\SpecialCharTok{+}
\NormalTok{                     inconsistent\_4 }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ inconsistent\_5 }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ inconsistent\_6}
\NormalTok{  )}

\CommentTok{\# Distribution of inconsistent items}
\FunctionTok{table}\NormalTok{(df\_primary}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{n\_inconsistent)}

\subsubsection{As the scale items were adapted for this study, EFA is
used to understand associations between all the factors. Parallel
analysis is used to determine the number of factors for each scales. It
compares eigenvalues from real data against random data of the same size
and retains factors that explain more variance than
chance.}\label{as-the-scale-items-were-adapted-for-this-study-efa-is-used-to-understand-associations-between-all-the-factors.-parallel-analysis-is-used-to-determine-the-number-of-factors-for-each-scales.-it-compares-eigenvalues-from-real-data-against-random-data-of-the-same-size-and-retains-factors-that-explain-more-variance-than-chance.}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Select items}
\NormalTok{ai\_use\_items }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ df\_primary }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%} \FunctionTok{select}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{starts\_with}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"ai\_use"}\NormalTok{))}
\NormalTok{ai\_disclosure\_items }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ df\_primary }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%} \FunctionTok{select}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{starts\_with}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"ai\_disclosure"}\NormalTok{))}

\CommentTok{\# Parallel analysis}
\FunctionTok{fa.parallel}\NormalTok{(ai\_use\_items, }\AttributeTok{fm =} \StringTok{"pa"}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{fa =} \StringTok{"fa"}\NormalTok{, }
            \AttributeTok{main =} \StringTok{"Parallel Analysis {-} AI Use"}\NormalTok{)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

            \AttributeTok{main =} \StringTok{"Parallel Analysis {-} AI Disclosure"}\NormalTok{)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsubsection{Which items cluster together onto which factors, which
then tells you whether your scale items are measuring one thing or
multiple distinct
dimensions.}\label{which-items-cluster-together-onto-which-factors-which-then-tells-you-whether-your-scale-items-are-measuring-one-thing-or-multiple-distinct-dimensions.}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Principal axis factoring (fm = "pa") with oblimin rotation (oblique) allows factors to correlate, appropriate for psychological constructs. The cutoff = 0.3.}

\CommentTok{\# AI Use {-} 3 factor solution (parallel analysis suggestion)}
\NormalTok{efa\_ai\_use\_3 }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{fa}\NormalTok{(ai\_use\_items, }\AttributeTok{nfactors =} \DecValTok{3}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{fm =} \StringTok{"pa"}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{rotate =} \StringTok{"oblimin"}\NormalTok{)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{verbatim}
## maximum iteration exceeded
\end{verbatim}

\begin{verbatim}
## Loading required namespace: GPArotation
\end{verbatim}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\FunctionTok{print}\NormalTok{(efa\_ai\_use\_3}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{loadings, }\AttributeTok{cutoff =} \FloatTok{0.3}\NormalTok{)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsection{Factor loading tables}\label{factor-loading-tables}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Extract loadings for 1{-}factor ai\_use}
\NormalTok{loadings\_use }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{as.data.frame}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{unclass}\NormalTok{(efa\_ai\_use\_1}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{loadings)) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{round}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{3}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{rownames\_to\_column}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"Item"}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}
    \AttributeTok{Item =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"Brainstorming ideas and refining topic"}\NormalTok{,}
             \StringTok{"Conducting literature review"}\NormalTok{,}
             \StringTok{"Rewriting paragraphs or sentences"}\NormalTok{,}
             \StringTok{"Evaluating or assessing writing"}\NormalTok{,}
             \StringTok{"Writing parts of essay"}\NormalTok{,}
             \StringTok{"Summarising without reading source"}\NormalTok{),}
    \AttributeTok{Scale =} \StringTok{"AI Use"}
\NormalTok{  )}
\FunctionTok{names}\NormalTok{(loadings\_use)[}\DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{] }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \StringTok{"Factor 1"}

\CommentTok{\# Replace small loadings with blank}
\NormalTok{loadings\_use[}\StringTok{"Factor 1"}\NormalTok{] }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{lapply}\NormalTok{(loadings\_use[}\StringTok{"Factor 1"}\NormalTok{],}
                                    \ControlFlowTok{function}\NormalTok{(x) }\FunctionTok{ifelse}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{abs}\NormalTok{(x) }\SpecialCharTok{\textless{}} \FloatTok{0.3}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{""}\NormalTok{, x))}

\CommentTok{\# Extract loadings for 2{-}factor ai\_disclosure}
\NormalTok{loadings\_disclosure }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{as.data.frame}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{unclass}\NormalTok{(efa\_ai\_disclosure\_2}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{loadings)) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{round}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{3}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{rownames\_to\_column}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"Item"}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}
    \AttributeTok{Item =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"Disclosing brainstorming ideas and refining topic"}\NormalTok{,}
             \StringTok{"Disclosing conducting literature review"}\NormalTok{,}
             \StringTok{"Disclosing rewriting paragraphs or sentences"}\NormalTok{,}
             \StringTok{"Disclosing evaluating or assessing writing"}\NormalTok{,}
             \StringTok{"Disclosing writing parts of essay"}\NormalTok{,}
             \StringTok{"Disclosing summarising without reading source"}\NormalTok{),}
    \AttributeTok{Scale =} \StringTok{"AI Disclosure"}
\NormalTok{  )}
\FunctionTok{names}\NormalTok{(loadings\_disclosure)[}\DecValTok{2}\SpecialCharTok{:}\DecValTok{3}\NormalTok{] }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"Factor 1"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"Factor 2"}\NormalTok{)}

\CommentTok{\# Replace small loadings with blank}
\NormalTok{loadings\_disclosure[}\FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"Factor 1"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"Factor 2"}\NormalTok{)] }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{lapply}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{  loadings\_disclosure[}\FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"Factor 1"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"Factor 2"}\NormalTok{)],}
  \ControlFlowTok{function}\NormalTok{(x) }\FunctionTok{ifelse}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{abs}\NormalTok{(x) }\SpecialCharTok{\textless{}} \FloatTok{0.3}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{""}\NormalTok{, x))}

\CommentTok{\# Print tables}
\NormalTok{loadings\_use }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{select}\NormalTok{(Scale, Item, }\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\AttributeTok{Factor 1}\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{gt}\NormalTok{() }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{tab\_header}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{title =} \StringTok{"Table 4. EFA Factor Loadings {-} AI Use (1 factor)"}\NormalTok{)}

\subsection{AI disclosure shows a 2-factor structure - single composites
for the primary analysis for parsimony. Future studies can explore these
subscales.}\label{ai-disclosure-shows-a-2-factor-structure---single-composites-for-the-primary-analysis-for-parsimony.-future-studies-can-explore-these-subscales.}

\subsection{Discussion - theoretical implications - disclosure
intentions are not the same across all items, they cluster into two
groups - group 1 (Rewriting paragraphs or sentences, writing parts of
your essay and summarising without reading the source) \& group 2
(Brainstorming and refining topic, Conducting literature review,
Evaluating or assessing your writing). Participants respond differently
to both, which suggests if they are confirmed, they could have
implications on the design of policies. {[}Use
Qual{]}}\label{discussion---theoretical-implications---disclosure-intentions-are-not-the-same-across-all-items-they-cluster-into-two-groups---group-1-rewriting-paragraphs-or-sentences-writing-parts-of-your-essay-and-summarising-without-reading-the-source-group-2-brainstorming-and-refining-topic-conducting-literature-review-evaluating-or-assessing-your-writing.-participants-respond-differently-to-both-which-suggests-if-they-are-confirmed-they-could-have-implications-on-the-design-of-policies.-use-qual}

\subsubsection{Composites}\label{composites}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Single composite score as mean of all 6 items for each scale}
\CommentTok{\# Justified by 1{-}factor EFA solution and acceptable Cronbach\textquotesingle{}s alpha}

  \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}
    \AttributeTok{ai\_use\_composite =} \FunctionTok{rowMeans}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{select}\NormalTok{(., ai\_use\_1}\SpecialCharTok{:}\NormalTok{ai\_use\_6), }\AttributeTok{na.rm =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{),}
    \AttributeTok{ai\_disclosure\_composite =} \FunctionTok{rowMeans}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{select}\NormalTok{(., ai\_disclosure\_1}\SpecialCharTok{:}\NormalTok{ai\_disclosure\_6), }\AttributeTok{na.rm =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{  )}

\end{Highlighting}

\subsubsection{Outliers check using

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Participants with |z| \textgreater{} 3.29 considered outliers (p \textless{} .001, two{-}tailed)}
\NormalTok{df\_primary }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ df\_primary }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}
    \AttributeTok{z\_ai\_use =} \FunctionTok{as.numeric}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{scale}\NormalTok{(ai\_use\_composite)),}
    \AttributeTok{z\_ai\_disclosure =} \FunctionTok{as.numeric}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{scale}\NormalTok{(ai\_disclosure\_composite))}
\NormalTok{  )}

\CommentTok{\# Identify outliers}
\NormalTok{outliers }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ df\_primary }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{filter}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{abs}\NormalTok{(z\_ai\_use) }\SpecialCharTok{\textgreater{}} \FloatTok{3.29} \SpecialCharTok{|} \FunctionTok{abs}\NormalTok{(z\_ai\_disclosure) }\SpecialCharTok{\textgreater{}} \FloatTok{3.29}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{select}\NormalTok{(response\_id, condition, ai\_use\_composite, z\_ai\_use,}
\NormalTok{         ai\_disclosure\_composite, z\_ai\_disclosure)}

\FunctionTok{print}\NormalTok{(outliers)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsubsection{Manipulation Check}\label{manipulation-check}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# mancheck1: 1=hierarchical[AR], 2=reciprocal[EM], 3=merit{-}based[MP]}
\CommentTok{\# mancheck2: 1=equals[EM], 2=institutional power[AR], 3=evaluation[MP]}

\NormalTok{mancheck\_summary }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ df\_primary }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{group\_by}\NormalTok{(condition) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{summarise}\NormalTok{(}
    \AttributeTok{mc1\_hierarchical =} \FunctionTok{mean}\NormalTok{(mancheck1\_1, }\AttributeTok{na.rm =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{),}
    \AttributeTok{mc1\_reciprocal   =} \FunctionTok{mean}\NormalTok{(mancheck1\_2, }\AttributeTok{na.rm =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{),}
    \AttributeTok{mc1\_merit        =} \FunctionTok{mean}\NormalTok{(mancheck1\_3, }\AttributeTok{na.rm =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{),}
    \AttributeTok{mc2\_equals       =} \FunctionTok{mean}\NormalTok{(mancheck2\_1, }\AttributeTok{na.rm =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{),}
    \AttributeTok{mc2\_power        =} \FunctionTok{mean}\NormalTok{(mancheck2\_2, }\AttributeTok{na.rm =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{),}
    \AttributeTok{mc2\_evaluation   =} \FunctionTok{mean}\NormalTok{(mancheck2\_3, }\AttributeTok{na.rm =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{  )}

\NormalTok{mancheck\_summary }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{gt}\NormalTok{() }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{tab\_header}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{title =} \StringTok{"Table 6. Manipulation Check Means by Condition"}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{cols\_label}\NormalTok{(}
    \AttributeTok{condition        =} \StringTok{"Condition"}\NormalTok{,}
    \AttributeTok{mc1\_hierarchical =} \StringTok{"Hierarchical"}\NormalTok{,}
    \AttributeTok{mc1\_reciprocal   =} \StringTok{"Reciprocal"}\NormalTok{,}
    \AttributeTok{mc1\_merit        =} \StringTok{"Merit{-}based"}\NormalTok{,}
    \AttributeTok{mc2\_equals       =} \StringTok{"Equals"}\NormalTok{,}
    \AttributeTok{mc2\_power        =} \StringTok{"Institutional Power"}\NormalTok{,}
    \AttributeTok{mc2\_evaluation   =} \StringTok{"Evaluation"}
\NormalTok{  ) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{tab\_spanner}\NormalTok{(}
    \AttributeTok{label =} \StringTok{"Policy Perception (mancheck1)"}\NormalTok{,}
    \AttributeTok{columns =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(mc1\_hierarchical, mc1\_reciprocal, mc1\_merit)}
\NormalTok{  ) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{tab\_spanner}\NormalTok{(}
    \AttributeTok{label =} \StringTok{"Declaration Perception (mancheck2)"}\NormalTok{,}
    \AttributeTok{columns =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(mc2\_equals, mc2\_power, mc2\_evaluation)}
\NormalTok{  ) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{fmt\_number}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{decimals =} \DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

differ significantly on each
item}\label{anova-on-manipulation-check-items---do-condition-means-differ-significantly-on-each-item}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# mancheck1 {-} policy perception}
\NormalTok{anova\_mc1\_hierarchical }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{aov}\NormalTok{(mancheck1\_1 }\SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}}\NormalTok{ condition, }\AttributeTok{data =}\NormalTok{ df\_primary)}
\NormalTok{anova\_mc1\_reciprocal   }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{aov}\NormalTok{(mancheck1\_2 }\SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}}\NormalTok{ condition, }\AttributeTok{data =}\NormalTok{ df\_primary)}
\NormalTok{anova\_mc1\_merit        }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{aov}\NormalTok{(mancheck1\_3 }\SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}}\NormalTok{ condition, }\AttributeTok{data =}\NormalTok{ df\_primary)}

\CommentTok{\# mancheck2 {-} declaration perception}
\NormalTok{anova\_mc2\_equals       }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{aov}\NormalTok{(mancheck2\_1 }\SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}}\NormalTok{ condition, }\AttributeTok{data =}\NormalTok{ df\_primary)}
\NormalTok{anova\_mc2\_power        }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{aov}\NormalTok{(mancheck2\_2 }\SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}}\NormalTok{ condition, }\AttributeTok{data =}\NormalTok{ df\_primary)}
\NormalTok{anova\_mc2\_evaluation   }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{aov}\NormalTok{(mancheck2\_3 }\SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}}\NormalTok{ condition, }\AttributeTok{data =}\NormalTok{ df\_primary)}

\subsubsection{Grouped bar chart - manipulation check means by condition
and
item}\label{grouped-bar-chart---manipulation-check-means-by-condition-and-item}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\NormalTok{mancheck\_long }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ mancheck\_summary }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{condition =} \FunctionTok{factor}\NormalTok{(condition, }\AttributeTok{levels =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"AR"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"EM"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"MP"}\NormalTok{))) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{pivot\_longer}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{cols =} \SpecialCharTok{{-}}\NormalTok{condition,}
               \AttributeTok{names\_to =} \StringTok{"item"}\NormalTok{,}
               \AttributeTok{values\_to =} \StringTok{"mean"}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}
    \AttributeTok{check =} \FunctionTok{case\_when}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{      item }\SpecialCharTok{\%in\%} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"mc1\_hierarchical"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"mc1\_reciprocal"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"mc1\_merit"}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \StringTok{"Policy Perception"}\NormalTok{,}
      \ConstantTok{TRUE} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \StringTok{"Declaration Perception"}
\NormalTok{    ),}
    \AttributeTok{item\_label =} \FunctionTok{case\_when}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{      item }\SpecialCharTok{==} \StringTok{"mc1\_hierarchical"} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \StringTok{"Hierarchical [AR]"}\NormalTok{,}
\NormalTok{      item }\SpecialCharTok{==} \StringTok{"mc1\_reciprocal"}   \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \StringTok{"Reciprocal [EM]"}\NormalTok{,}
\NormalTok{      item }\SpecialCharTok{==} \StringTok{"mc1\_merit"}        \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \StringTok{"Merit{-}based [MP]"}\NormalTok{,}
\NormalTok{      item }\SpecialCharTok{==} \StringTok{"mc2\_equals"}       \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \StringTok{"Equals [EM]"}\NormalTok{,}
\NormalTok{      item }\SpecialCharTok{==} \StringTok{"mc2\_power"}        \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \StringTok{"Institutional Power [AR]"}\NormalTok{,}
\NormalTok{      item }\SpecialCharTok{==} \StringTok{"mc2\_evaluation"}   \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \StringTok{"Evaluation [MP]"}
\NormalTok{    )}
\NormalTok{  )}

\FunctionTok{ggplot}\NormalTok{(mancheck\_long, }\FunctionTok{aes}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{x =}\NormalTok{ item\_label, }\AttributeTok{y =}\NormalTok{ mean, }\AttributeTok{fill =}\NormalTok{ condition)) }\SpecialCharTok{+}
  \FunctionTok{geom\_bar}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{stat =} \StringTok{"identity"}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{position =} \StringTok{"dodge"}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{width =} \FloatTok{0.6}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{+}
  \FunctionTok{geom\_hline}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{yintercept =} \DecValTok{3}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{linetype =} \StringTok{"dashed"}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{colour =} \StringTok{"grey50"}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{+}
  \FunctionTok{facet\_wrap}\NormalTok{(}\SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}}\NormalTok{check, }\AttributeTok{scales =} \StringTok{"free\_x"}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{+}
  \FunctionTok{scale\_fill\_manual}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{values =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"AR"} \OtherTok{=} \StringTok{"\#2196F3"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"EM"} \OtherTok{=} \StringTok{"\#F44336"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"MP"} \OtherTok{=} \StringTok{"\#4CAF50"}\NormalTok{)) }\SpecialCharTok{+}
  \FunctionTok{labs}\NormalTok{(}
    \AttributeTok{title =} \StringTok{"Figure 1. Manipulation Check Means by Condition and Item"}\NormalTok{,}
    \AttributeTok{x =} \ConstantTok{NULL}\NormalTok{,}
    \AttributeTok{y =} \StringTok{"Mean Rating (1{-}5)"}\NormalTok{,}
    \AttributeTok{fill =} \StringTok{"Condition"}\NormalTok{,}
    \AttributeTok{caption =} \StringTok{"Dashed line indicates scale midpoint (3.0)"}
\NormalTok{  ) }\SpecialCharTok{+}
  \FunctionTok{theme\_minimal}\NormalTok{() }\SpecialCharTok{+}
  \FunctionTok{theme}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{axis.text.x =} \FunctionTok{element\_text}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{angle =} \DecValTok{45}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{hjust =} \DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{)) }\SpecialCharTok{+}
  \FunctionTok{coord\_cartesian}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{ylim =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{5}\NormalTok{))}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\FunctionTok{abline}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{lm}\NormalTok{(ai\_disclosure\_composite }\SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}}\NormalTok{ english\_proficiency, }\AttributeTok{data =}\NormalTok{ df\_primary), }\AttributeTok{col =} \StringTok{"red"}\NormalTok{)}

\subsubsection{PRIMARY ANALYSIS}\label{primary-analysis}

\subsubsection{One-way ANOVA - Tests whether there are significant
differences in means across the
three}\label{one-way-anova---tests-whether-there-are-significant-differences-in-means-across-the-three}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# relational framing conditions (AR, EM, MP) for intended AI use and disclosure}
\CommentTok{\# Omnibus test {-} does not test specific pairwise differences}
\CommentTok{\# Addresses RQ1 (AI use) and RQ2 (disclosure)}

\NormalTok{df\_primary }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ df\_primary }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{condition =} \FunctionTok{factor}\NormalTok{(condition, }\AttributeTok{levels =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"AR"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"EM"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"MP"}\NormalTok{)))}

\CommentTok{\# RQ1: AI use across conditions}
\NormalTok{anova\_use }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{aov}\NormalTok{(ai\_use\_composite }\SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}}\NormalTok{ condition, }\AttributeTok{data =}\NormalTok{ df\_primary)}
\FunctionTok{summary}\NormalTok{(anova\_use)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{verbatim}
##              Df Sum Sq Mean Sq F value Pr(>F)
## condition     2   0.01  0.0068   0.008  0.992
## Residuals   198 158.99  0.8030
\end{verbatim}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# RQ2: AI disclosure across conditions}
\NormalTok{anova\_disclosure }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{aov}\NormalTok{(ai\_disclosure\_composite }\SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}}\NormalTok{ condition, }\AttributeTok{data =}\NormalTok{ df\_primary)}
\FunctionTok{summary}\NormalTok{(anova\_disclosure)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{verbatim}
##              Df Sum Sq Mean Sq F value Pr(>F)  
## condition     2   6.21   3.106   2.824 0.0618 .
## Residuals   198 217.81   1.100                 
## ---
## Signif. codes:  0 '***' 0.001 '**' 0.01 '*' 0.05 '.' 0.1 ' ' 1
\end{verbatim}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Group means and SDs}
\NormalTok{df\_primary }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{group\_by}\NormalTok{(condition) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{summarise}\NormalTok{(}
    \AttributeTok{mean\_use        =} \FunctionTok{mean}\NormalTok{(ai\_use\_composite, }\AttributeTok{na.rm =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{),}
    \AttributeTok{sd\_use          =} \FunctionTok{sd}\NormalTok{(ai\_use\_composite, }\AttributeTok{na.rm =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{),}
    \AttributeTok{mean\_disclosure =} \FunctionTok{mean}\NormalTok{(ai\_disclosure\_composite, }\AttributeTok{na.rm =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{),}
    \AttributeTok{sd\_disclosure   =} \FunctionTok{sd}\NormalTok{(ai\_disclosure\_composite, }\AttributeTok{na.rm =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{  )}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{verbatim}
## # A tibble: 3 x 5
##   condition mean_use sd_use mean_disclosure sd_disclosure
##   <fct>        <dbl>  <dbl>           <dbl>         <dbl>
## 1 AR            3.04  0.907            2.50          1.10
## 2 EM            3.02  0.825            2.12          1.00
## 3 MP            3.03  0.952            2.47          1.05
\end{verbatim}

\subsection{Tukey's}\label{tukeys}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Only run for disclosure since AI use ANOVA was non{-}significant}
\NormalTok{tukey\_disclosure }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{TukeyHSD}\NormalTok{(anova\_disclosure)}
\FunctionTok{print}\NormalTok{(tukey\_disclosure)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{verbatim}
##   Tukey multiple comparisons of means
##     95% family-wise confidence level
## 
## Fit: aov(formula = ai_disclosure_composite ~ condition, data = df_primary)
## 
## $condition
##              diff         lwr       upr     p adj
## EM-AR -0.38805970 -0.81598430 0.0398649 0.0842096
## MP-AR -0.03233831 -0.46026291 0.3955863 0.9825979
## MP-EM  0.35572139 -0.07220321 0.7836460 0.1241680
\end{verbatim}

\subsection{Regression model 1
(covariates)}\label{regression-model-1-covariates}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Step 1 of hierarchical regression}
\CommentTok{\# Tests whether English proficiency and UAI predict disclosure and AI use}
\NormalTok{model1\_use }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{lm}\NormalTok{(ai\_use\_composite }\SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}}\NormalTok{ english\_proficiency }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ uai,}\AttributeTok{data =}\NormalTok{ df\_primary)}
\FunctionTok{summary}\NormalTok{(model1\_use)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{verbatim}

## english_proficiency -0.221048   0.185072  -1.194    0.234    
## uai                 -0.003909   0.005538  -0.706    0.481    
## ---
## Signif. codes:  0 '***' 0.001 '**' 0.01 '*' 0.05 '.' 0.1 ' ' 1
## 
## Residual standard error: 0.8937 on 196 degrees of freedom
##   (2 observations deleted due to missingness)
## Multiple R-squared:  0.007226,   Adjusted R-squared:  -0.002904 
## F-statistic: 0.7133 on 2 and 196 DF,  p-value: 0.4913
\end{verbatim}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\NormalTok{model1\_disclosure }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{lm}\NormalTok{(ai\_disclosure\_composite }\SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}}\NormalTok{ english\_proficiency }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ uai, }\AttributeTok{data =}\NormalTok{ df\_primary)}
\FunctionTok{summary}\NormalTok{(model1\_disclosure)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{verbatim}

\section{VIF check - more predictors means more potential for
multicollinearity}\label{vif-check---more-predictors-means-more-potential-for-multicollinearity}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# VIF {-} multicollinearity between predictors (VIF \textless{} 10 acceptable)}
\CommentTok{\# Breusch{-}Pagan {-} homoscedasticity of residuals (p \textgreater{} .05 = assumption holds)}

\CommentTok{\# VIF}
\FunctionTok{cat}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"=== VIF {-} AI Disclosure Model ===}\SpecialCharTok{\textbackslash{}n}\StringTok{"}\NormalTok{)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{verbatim}
## === VIF - AI Disclosure Model ===
\end{verbatim}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\FunctionTok{vif}\NormalTok{(model2\_disclosure)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{verbatim}

## english_proficiency 1.577869  1        1.256132
## uai                 1.571890  1        1.253750
## condition           1.021681  2        1.005377
\end{verbatim}

\begin{Highlighting}[]
\FunctionTok{cat}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"}\SpecialCharTok{\textbackslash{}n}\StringTok{=== VIF {-} AI Use Model ===}\SpecialCharTok{\textbackslash{}n}\StringTok{"}\NormalTok{)}

\subsection{Bootstrapping}\label{bootstrapping}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# 5000 samples to account for non{-}normal distribution of ai\_disclosure}
\CommentTok{\# AR as reference category (pre{-}registered)}
\NormalTok{boot\_ar }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \ControlFlowTok{function}\NormalTok{(data, indices) \{}
\NormalTok{  d }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ data[indices, ]}
\NormalTok{  fit }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{lm}\NormalTok{(ai\_disclosure\_composite }\SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}}\NormalTok{ english\_proficiency }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ uai }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ condition,}
            \AttributeTok{data =}\NormalTok{ d)}
  \FunctionTok{return}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{coef}\NormalTok{(fit))}
\NormalTok{\}}

\FunctionTok{set.seed}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{123}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{boot\_ar\_results }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{boot}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{data =}\NormalTok{ df\_primary, }\AttributeTok{statistic =}\NormalTok{ boot\_ar, }\AttributeTok{R =} \DecValTok{5000}\NormalTok{)}

\CommentTok{\# CIs for each coefficient}
\FunctionTok{boot.ci}\NormalTok{(boot\_ar\_results, }\AttributeTok{type =} \StringTok{"perc"}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{index =} \DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{) }\CommentTok{\# intercept}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{verbatim}
## BOOTSTRAP CONFIDENCE INTERVAL CALCULATIONS
## Based on 5000 bootstrap replicates
## 
## CALL : 
## boot.ci(boot.out = boot_ar_results, type = "perc", index = 1)
## 
## Intervals : 
## Level     Percentile     
## 95%   ( 1.601,  5.979 )  
## Calculations and Intervals on Original Scale
\end{verbatim}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\FunctionTok{boot.ci}\NormalTok{(boot\_ar\_results, }\AttributeTok{type =} \StringTok{"perc"}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{index =} \DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{) }\CommentTok{\# english proficiency}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{verbatim}
## BOOTSTRAP CONFIDENCE INTERVAL CALCULATIONS
## Based on 5000 bootstrap replicates
## 
## CALL : 
## boot.ci(boot.out = boot_ar_results, type = "perc", index = 2)
## 
## Intervals : 
## Level     Percentile     
## 95%   (-0.6959,  0.2262 )  
## Calculations and Intervals on Original Scale
\end{verbatim}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\FunctionTok{boot.ci}\NormalTok{(boot\_ar\_results, }\AttributeTok{type =} \StringTok{"perc"}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{index =} \DecValTok{3}\NormalTok{) }\CommentTok{\# UAI}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{verbatim}
## BOOTSTRAP CONFIDENCE INTERVAL CALCULATIONS
## Based on 5000 bootstrap replicates
## 
## CALL : 
## boot.ci(boot.out = boot_ar_results, type = "perc", index = 3)
## 
## Intervals : 
## Level     Percentile     
## 95%   (-0.0241,  0.0019 )  
## Calculations and Intervals on Original Scale
\end{verbatim}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\FunctionTok{boot.ci}\NormalTok{(boot\_ar\_results, }\AttributeTok{type =} \StringTok{"perc"}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{index =} \DecValTok{4}\NormalTok{) }\CommentTok{\# EM vs AR}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{verbatim}
## BOOTSTRAP CONFIDENCE INTERVAL CALCULATIONS
## Based on 5000 bootstrap replicates
## 
## CALL : 
## boot.ci(boot.out = boot_ar_results, type = "perc", index = 4)
## 
## Intervals : 
## Level     Percentile     
## 95%   (-0.7498, -0.0331 )  
## Calculations and Intervals on Original Scale
\end{verbatim}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\FunctionTok{boot.ci}\NormalTok{(boot\_ar\_results, }\AttributeTok{type =} \StringTok{"perc"}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{index =} \DecValTok{5}\NormalTok{) }\CommentTok{\# MP vs AR}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{verbatim}
## BOOTSTRAP CONFIDENCE INTERVAL CALCULATIONS
## Based on 5000 bootstrap replicates
## 
## CALL : 
## boot.ci(boot.out = boot_ar_results, type = "perc", index = 5)
## 
## Intervals : 
## Level     Percentile     
## 95%   (-0.377,  0.348 )  
## Calculations and Intervals on Original Scale
\end{verbatim}

\subsubsection{Supplementary analysis - EM as reference
category}\label{supplementary-analysis---em-as-reference-category}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# EM as reference category to test AR vs EM and MP vs EM }

\NormalTok{df\_primary }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ df\_primary }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{condition\_em\_ref =} \FunctionTok{factor}\NormalTok{(condition, }\AttributeTok{levels =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"EM"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"AR"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"MP"}\NormalTok{)))}

\NormalTok{model2\_disclosure\_em }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{lm}\NormalTok{(ai\_disclosure\_composite }\SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}}\NormalTok{ english\_proficiency }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ uai }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ condition\_em\_ref, }\AttributeTok{data =}\NormalTok{ df\_primary)}
\FunctionTok{summary}\NormalTok{(model2\_disclosure\_em)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{verbatim}
## 
## Call:
## lm(formula = ai_disclosure_composite ~ english_proficiency + 
##     uai + condition_em_ref, data = df_primary)
## 
## Residuals:
##     Min      1Q  Median      3Q     Max 
## -1.5161 -0.7827 -0.1719  0.6614  2.8839 
## 
## Coefficients:
##                     Estimate Std. Error t value Pr(>|t|)    
## (Intercept)          3.65374    1.02278   3.572 0.000446 ***
## english_proficiency -0.28863    0.21966  -1.314 0.190400    
## uai                 -0.01095    0.00656  -1.669 0.096803 .  
## condition_em_refAR   0.40006    0.18241   2.193 0.029485 *  
## condition_em_refMP   0.38916    0.18402   2.115 0.035725 *  
## ---
## Signif. codes:  0 '***' 0.001 '**' 0.01 '*' 0.05 '.' 0.1 ' ' 1
## 
## Residual standard error: 1.051 on 194 degrees of freedom
##   (2 observations deleted due to missingness)
## Multiple R-squared:  0.04165,    Adjusted R-squared:  0.02189 
## F-statistic: 2.108 on 4 and 194 DF,  p-value: 0.08135
\end{verbatim}

\subsubsection{Bootstrapping - EM as
reference}\label{bootstrapping---em-as-reference}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\#Bootstrapped CIs {-} EM reference}
\CommentTok{\# 5,000 samples to account for non{-}normal residuals in ai\_disclosure}

\NormalTok{boot\_em }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \ControlFlowTok{function}\NormalTok{(data, indices) \{}
\NormalTok{  d }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ data[indices, ]}
\NormalTok{  fit }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{lm}\NormalTok{(ai\_disclosure\_composite }\SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}}\NormalTok{ english\_proficiency }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ uai }\SpecialCharTok{+}
\NormalTok{              condition\_em\_ref, }\AttributeTok{data =}\NormalTok{ d)}
  \FunctionTok{return}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{coef}\NormalTok{(fit))}
\NormalTok{\}}

\FunctionTok{set.seed}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{123}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{boot\_em\_results }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{boot}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{data =}\NormalTok{ df\_primary, }\AttributeTok{statistic =}\NormalTok{ boot\_em, }\AttributeTok{R =} \DecValTok{5000}\NormalTok{)}

\FunctionTok{boot.ci}\NormalTok{(boot\_em\_results, }\AttributeTok{type =} \StringTok{"perc"}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{index =} \DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{) }\CommentTok{\# intercept}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{verbatim}
## BOOTSTRAP CONFIDENCE INTERVAL CALCULATIONS
## Based on 5000 bootstrap replicates
## 
## CALL : 
## boot.ci(boot.out = boot_em_results, type = "perc", index = 1)
##

## Level     Percentile     
## 95%   ( 1.260,  5.632 )  
## Calculations and Intervals on Original Scale
\end{verbatim}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Mixed ANOVA for manipulation check {-} Policy Perception (mancheck1)}
\CommentTok{\# Within{-}subject factor: relational dimension (hierarchical, reciprocal, merit)}
\CommentTok{\# Between{-}subject factor: condition (AR, EM, MP)}

\FunctionTok{library}\NormalTok{(tidyr)}
\FunctionTok{library}\NormalTok{(dplyr)}

\CommentTok{\# Reshape to long format}
\NormalTok{mancheck\_long\_mixed }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ df\_primary }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{select}\NormalTok{(response\_id, condition, mancheck1\_1, mancheck1\_2, mancheck1\_3) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{pivot\_longer}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{cols =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(mancheck1\_1, mancheck1\_2, mancheck1\_3),}
               \AttributeTok{names\_to =} \StringTok{"dimension"}\NormalTok{,}
               \AttributeTok{values\_to =} \StringTok{"rating"}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{dimension =} \FunctionTok{case\_when}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{    dimension }\SpecialCharTok{==} \StringTok{"mancheck1\_1"} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \StringTok{"Hierarchical"}\NormalTok{,}
\NormalTok{    dimension }\SpecialCharTok{==} \StringTok{"mancheck1\_2"} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \StringTok{"Reciprocal"}\NormalTok{,}
\NormalTok{    dimension }\SpecialCharTok{==} \StringTok{"mancheck1\_3"} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \StringTok{"Merit{-}based"}
\NormalTok{  ))}

\CommentTok{\# Mixed ANOVA}
\ControlFlowTok{if}\NormalTok{ (}\SpecialCharTok{!}\FunctionTok{requireNamespace}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"ez"}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{quietly =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{)) \{}
  \FunctionTok{install.packages}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"ez"}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{repos =} \StringTok{"https://cran.rstudio.com/"}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{\}}
\FunctionTok{library}\NormalTok{(ez)}
\NormalTok{mixed\_anova\_mc1 }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{ezANOVA}\NormalTok{(}
  \AttributeTok{data =}\NormalTok{ mancheck\_long\_mixed,}
  \AttributeTok{dv =}\NormalTok{ rating,}
  \AttributeTok{wid =}\NormalTok{ response\_id,}
  \AttributeTok{within =}\NormalTok{ dimension,}
  \AttributeTok{between =}\NormalTok{ condition,}
  \AttributeTok{type =} \DecValTok{3}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{return\_aov =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}
\NormalTok{)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{verbatim}
## Warning: Converting "response_id" to factor for ANOVA.
\end{verbatim}

\begin{verbatim}
## Warning: Converting "dimension" to factor for ANOVA.
\end{verbatim}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\FunctionTok{print}\NormalTok{(mixed\_anova\_mc1)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{verbatim}
## $ANOVA
##                Effect DFn DFd         F          p p<.05         ges
## 2           condition   2 198 0.2436869 0.78396771       0.001231064
## 3           dimension   2 396 0.7775731 0.46022017       0.001956797
## 4 condition:dimension   4 396 2.1756850 0.07103854       0.010852811
## 
## $`Mauchly's Test for Sphericity`
##                Effect         W           p p<.05
## 3           dimension 0.9404077 0.002353126     *
## 4 condition:dimension 0.9404077 0.002353126     *
## 
## $`Sphericity Corrections`
##                Effect       GGe      p[GG] p[GG]<.05       HFe      p[HF]
## 3           dimension 0.9437593 0.45353730           0.9525558 0.45460944
## 4 condition:dimension 0.9437593 0.07508766           0.9525558 0.07443871
##   p[HF]<.05
## 3          
## 4          
## 
## $aov
## 
## Call:
## aov(formula = formula(aov_formula), data = data)
## 
## Grand Mean: 3.046434
## 
## Stratum 1: response_id
## 
## Terms:
##                 condition Residuals
## Sum of Squares    0.64013 260.05970
## Deg. of Freedom         2       198
## 
## Residual standard error: 1.146051
## 4 out of 6 effects not estimable
## Estimated effects may be unbalanced
## 
## Stratum 2: response_id:dimension
## 
## Terms:
##                 dimension condition:dimension Residuals
## Sum of Squares    1.01824             5.69818 259.28358
## Deg. of Freedom         2                   4       396
## 
## Residual standard error: 0.8091703
## Estimated effects may be unbalanced
\end{verbatim}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Mixed ANOVA for manipulation check {-} Declaration Perception (mancheck2)}
\CommentTok{\# Within{-}subject factor: relational dimension (equals, institutional power, evaluation)}
\CommentTok{\# Between{-}subject factor: condition (AR, EM, MP)}

\NormalTok{mancheck\_long\_mixed2 }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ df\_primary }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{select}\NormalTok{(response\_id, condition, mancheck2\_1, mancheck2\_2, mancheck2\_3) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{pivot\_longer}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{cols =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(mancheck2\_1, mancheck2\_2, mancheck2\_3),}
               \AttributeTok{names\_to =} \StringTok{"dimension"}\NormalTok{,}
               \AttributeTok{values\_to =} \StringTok{"rating"}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{dimension =} \FunctionTok{case\_when}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{    dimension }\SpecialCharTok{==} \StringTok{"mancheck2\_1"} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \StringTok{"Equals"}\NormalTok{,}
\NormalTok{    dimension }\SpecialCharTok{==} \StringTok{"mancheck2\_2"} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \StringTok{"Institutional Power"}\NormalTok{,}
\NormalTok{    dimension }\SpecialCharTok{==} \StringTok{"mancheck2\_3"} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \StringTok{"Evaluation"}
\NormalTok{  ))}

\CommentTok{\# Mixed ANOVA}
\NormalTok{mixed\_anova\_mc2 }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{ezANOVA}\NormalTok{(}
  \AttributeTok{data =}\NormalTok{ mancheck\_long\_mixed2,}
  \AttributeTok{dv =}\NormalTok{ rating,}
  \AttributeTok{wid =}\NormalTok{ response\_id,}
  \AttributeTok{within =}\NormalTok{ dimension,}
  \AttributeTok{between =}\NormalTok{ condition,}
  \AttributeTok{type =} \DecValTok{3}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{return\_aov =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}
\NormalTok{)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Step 3: Simple effects {-} Policy Perception (mancheck1)}
\CommentTok{\# Test whether each condition rated its matched item relatively highest}

\CommentTok{\# Policy perception simple effects by condition}
\FunctionTok{library}\NormalTok{(broom)}
\NormalTok{mc1\_simple }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ mancheck\_long\_mixed }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{group\_by}\NormalTok{(condition) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{do}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{tidy}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{aov}\NormalTok{(rating }\SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}}\NormalTok{ dimension, }\AttributeTok{data =}\NormalTok{ .))) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{filter}\NormalTok{(term }\SpecialCharTok{==} \StringTok{"dimension"}\NormalTok{)}

\FunctionTok{print}\NormalTok{(mc1\_simple)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{verbatim}
## # A tibble: 3 x 7
## # Groups:   condition [3]
##   condition term         df  sumsq meansq statistic p.value
##   <fct>     <chr>     <dbl>  <dbl>  <dbl>     <dbl>   <dbl>
## 1 AR        dimension     2 0.428  0.214     0.215   0.806 
## 2 EM        dimension     2 6.25   3.12      3.34    0.0375
## 3 MP        dimension     2 0.0398 0.0199    0.0287  0.972
\end{verbatim}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Pairwise comparisons within each condition for policy perception}
\NormalTok{mc1\_pairwise }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ mancheck\_long\_mixed }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{group\_by}\NormalTok{(condition) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{do}\NormalTok{(\{}
    \FunctionTok{pairwise.t.test}\NormalTok{(.}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{rating, .}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{dimension, }
                    \AttributeTok{paired =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{, }
                    \AttributeTok{p.adjust.method =} \StringTok{"bonferroni"}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
      \FunctionTok{tidy}\NormalTok{()}
\NormalTok{  \})}

\FunctionTok{print}\NormalTok{(mc1\_pairwise)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

  \FunctionTok{summarise}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{mean\_rating =} \FunctionTok{mean}\NormalTok{(rating, }\AttributeTok{na.rm =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{), }\AttributeTok{.groups =} \StringTok{"drop"}\NormalTok{)}

\FunctionTok{ggplot}\NormalTok{(mc1\_means, }\FunctionTok{aes}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{x =}\NormalTok{ dimension, }\AttributeTok{y =}\NormalTok{ mean\_rating, }

  \FunctionTok{group\_by}\NormalTok{(condition, dimension) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{summarise}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{mean\_rating =} \FunctionTok{mean}\NormalTok{(rating, }\AttributeTok{na.rm =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{), }\AttributeTok{.groups =} \StringTok{"drop"}\NormalTok{)}

\FunctionTok{ggplot}\NormalTok{(mc2\_means, }\FunctionTok{aes}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{x =}\NormalTok{ dimension, }\AttributeTok{y =}\NormalTok{ mean\_rating,}
                       \AttributeTok{group =}\NormalTok{ condition, }\AttributeTok{colour =}\NormalTok{ condition)) }\SpecialCharTok{+}

\subsubsection{Hypothesis 3 - Disclosure gap -
RQ3}\label{hypothesis-3---disclosure-gap---rq3}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Composite gap = ai\_use\_composite {-} ai\_disclosure\_composite}
\CommentTok{\# Item level gap = ai\_use item {-} ai\_disclosure item for each pair}
\CommentTok{\# Positive gap = participants intend to use AI more than they disclose}

\NormalTok{df\_primary }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ df\_primary }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}
    \AttributeTok{disclosure\_gap\_composite =}\NormalTok{ ai\_use\_composite }\SpecialCharTok{{-}}\NormalTok{ ai\_disclosure\_composite,}
    \AttributeTok{gap\_1 =}\NormalTok{ ai\_use\_1 }\SpecialCharTok{{-}}\NormalTok{ ai\_disclosure\_1, }\CommentTok{\# brainstorming}
    \AttributeTok{gap\_2 =}\NormalTok{ ai\_use\_2 }\SpecialCharTok{{-}}\NormalTok{ ai\_disclosure\_2, }\CommentTok{\# literature review}
    \AttributeTok{gap\_3 =}\NormalTok{ ai\_use\_3 }\SpecialCharTok{{-}}\NormalTok{ ai\_disclosure\_3, }\CommentTok{\# rewriting}
    \AttributeTok{gap\_4 =}\NormalTok{ ai\_use\_4 }\SpecialCharTok{{-}}\NormalTok{ ai\_disclosure\_4, }\CommentTok{\# evaluating}
    \AttributeTok{gap\_5 =}\NormalTok{ ai\_use\_5 }\SpecialCharTok{{-}}\NormalTok{ ai\_disclosure\_5, }\CommentTok{\# writing parts}
    \AttributeTok{gap\_6 =}\NormalTok{ ai\_use\_6 }\SpecialCharTok{{-}}\NormalTok{ ai\_disclosure\_6  }\CommentTok{\# summarising}
\NormalTok{  )}

\CommentTok{\# Composite gap summary}
\FunctionTok{summary}\NormalTok{(df\_primary}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{disclosure\_gap\_composite)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{verbatim}
##    Min. 1st Qu.  Median    Mean 3rd Qu.    Max. 
## -3.5000  0.0000  0.6667  0.6683  1.5000  3.5000
\end{verbatim}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Item level gaps}
\NormalTok{item\_gaps }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ df\_primary }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{summarise}\NormalTok{(}
    \AttributeTok{brainstorming     =} \FunctionTok{mean}\NormalTok{(gap\_1, }\AttributeTok{na.rm =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{),}
    \AttributeTok{literature\_review =} \FunctionTok{mean}\NormalTok{(gap\_2, }\AttributeTok{na.rm =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{),}
    \AttributeTok{rewriting         =} \FunctionTok{mean}\NormalTok{(gap\_3, }\AttributeTok{na.rm =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{),}
    \AttributeTok{evaluating        =} \FunctionTok{mean}\NormalTok{(gap\_4, }\AttributeTok{na.rm =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{),}
    \AttributeTok{writing\_parts     =} \FunctionTok{mean}\NormalTok{(gap\_5, }\AttributeTok{na.rm =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{),}
    \AttributeTok{summarising       =} \FunctionTok{mean}\NormalTok{(gap\_6, }\AttributeTok{na.rm =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{  ) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{pivot\_longer}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{everything}\NormalTok{(), }\AttributeTok{names\_to =} \StringTok{"item"}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{values\_to =} \StringTok{"mean\_gap"}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{arrange}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{desc}\NormalTok{(mean\_gap))}

\FunctionTok{print}\NormalTok{(item\_gaps)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{verbatim}
## # A tibble: 6 x 2
##   item              mean_gap
##   <chr>                <dbl>
## 1 evaluating           0.980
## 2 brainstorming        0.861
## 3 summarising          0.756
## 4 literature_review    0.716
## 5 rewriting            0.532
## 6 writing_parts        0.164
\end{verbatim}

\subsubsection{ANOVA intention-disclosure
gap}\label{anova-intention-disclosure-gap}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\NormalTok{anova\_gap }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{aov}\NormalTok{(disclosure\_gap\_composite }\SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}}\NormalTok{ condition, }\AttributeTok{data =}\NormalTok{ df\_primary)}
\FunctionTok{summary}\NormalTok{(anova\_gap)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{verbatim}
##              Df Sum Sq Mean Sq F value Pr(>F)
## condition     2   5.67   2.836   1.851   0.16
## Residuals   198 303.44   1.532
\end{verbatim}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Group means and SDs by condition}
\NormalTok{df\_primary }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{group\_by}\NormalTok{(condition) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{summarise}\NormalTok{(}
    \AttributeTok{mean\_gap =} \FunctionTok{mean}\NormalTok{(disclosure\_gap\_composite, }\AttributeTok{na.rm =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{),}
    \AttributeTok{sd\_gap   =} \FunctionTok{sd}\NormalTok{(disclosure\_gap\_composite, }\AttributeTok{na.rm =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{  )}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{verbatim}
## # A tibble: 3 x 3
##   condition mean_gap sd_gap
##   <fct>        <dbl>  <dbl>
## 1 AR           0.537   1.36
## 2 EM           0.905   1.23
## 3 MP           0.562   1.11
\end{verbatim}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\FunctionTok{t.test}\NormalTok{(df\_primary}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{ai\_use\_composite, }
\NormalTok{       df\_primary}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{ai\_disclosure\_composite, }
       \AttributeTok{paired =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{verbatim}
## 
##  Paired t-test
## 
## data:  df_primary$ai_use_composite and df_primary$ai_disclosure_composite
## t = 7.6216, df = 200, p-value = 9.858e-13
## alternative hypothesis: true mean difference is not equal to 0
## 95 percent confidence interval:
##  0.4954119 0.8412382
## sample estimates:
## mean difference 
##        0.668325
\end{verbatim}

\subsubsection{USE-DISCLOSURE Gap
Diagram}\label{use-disclosure-gap-diagram}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Disclosure gap visualisations}

\CommentTok{\# Data preparation}
\NormalTok{item\_gap\_data }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{data.frame}\NormalTok{(}
  \AttributeTok{item =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"Brainstorming"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"Literature Review"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"Rewriting"}\NormalTok{, }
           \StringTok{"Evaluating"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"Writing Parts"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"Summarising"}\NormalTok{),}
  \AttributeTok{ai\_use =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{mean}\NormalTok{(df\_primary}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{ai\_use\_1), }\FunctionTok{mean}\NormalTok{(df\_primary}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{ai\_use\_2),}
             \FunctionTok{mean}\NormalTok{(df\_primary}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{ai\_use\_3), }\FunctionTok{mean}\NormalTok{(df\_primary}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{ai\_use\_4),}
             \FunctionTok{mean}\NormalTok{(df\_primary}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{ai\_use\_5), }\FunctionTok{mean}\NormalTok{(df\_primary}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{ai\_use\_6)),}
  \AttributeTok{ai\_disclosure =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{mean}\NormalTok{(df\_primary}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{ai\_disclosure\_1), }\FunctionTok{mean}\NormalTok{(df\_primary}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{ai\_disclosure\_2),}
                    \FunctionTok{mean}\NormalTok{(df\_primary}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{ai\_disclosure\_3), }\FunctionTok{mean}\NormalTok{(df\_primary}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{ai\_disclosure\_4),}
                    \FunctionTok{mean}\NormalTok{(df\_primary}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{ai\_disclosure\_5), }\FunctionTok{mean}\NormalTok{(df\_primary}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{ai\_disclosure\_6))}
\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{gap =}\NormalTok{ ai\_use }\SpecialCharTok{{-}}\NormalTok{ ai\_disclosure) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{arrange}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{desc}\NormalTok{(gap))}

\CommentTok{\# 1. Paired bar chart}
\NormalTok{item\_gap\_long }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ item\_gap\_data }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{pivot\_longer}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{cols =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(ai\_use, ai\_disclosure),}
               \AttributeTok{names\_to =} \StringTok{"measure"}\NormalTok{,}
               \AttributeTok{values\_to =} \StringTok{"mean"}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{measure =} \FunctionTok{case\_when}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{    measure }\SpecialCharTok{==} \StringTok{"ai\_use"} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \StringTok{"Intended AI Use"}\NormalTok{,}
\NormalTok{    measure }\SpecialCharTok{==} \StringTok{"ai\_disclosure"} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \StringTok{"Intended Disclosure"}
\NormalTok{  ),}
  \AttributeTok{item =} \FunctionTok{factor}\NormalTok{(item, }\AttributeTok{levels =}\NormalTok{ item\_gap\_data}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{item))}

\FunctionTok{ggplot}\NormalTok{(item\_gap\_long, }\FunctionTok{aes}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{x =}\NormalTok{ item, }\AttributeTok{y =}\NormalTok{ mean, }\AttributeTok{fill =}\NormalTok{ measure)) }\SpecialCharTok{+}
  \FunctionTok{geom\_bar}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{stat =} \StringTok{"identity"}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{position =} \StringTok{"dodge"}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{width =} \FloatTok{0.6}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{+}
  \FunctionTok{scale\_fill\_manual}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{values =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"Intended AI Use"} \OtherTok{=} \StringTok{"\#2196F3"}\NormalTok{, }
                                \StringTok{"Intended Disclosure"} \OtherTok{=} \StringTok{"\#F44336"}\NormalTok{)) }\SpecialCharTok{+}
  \FunctionTok{labs}\NormalTok{(}
    \AttributeTok{title =} \StringTok{"Intended AI Use and Disclosure Intentions by Item"}\NormalTok{,}
    \AttributeTok{x =} \ConstantTok{NULL}\NormalTok{,}
    \AttributeTok{y =} \StringTok{"Mean Score (1{-}5)"}\NormalTok{,}
    \AttributeTok{fill =} \ConstantTok{NULL}\NormalTok{,}
\NormalTok{  ) }\SpecialCharTok{+}
 \FunctionTok{theme\_minimal}\NormalTok{() }\SpecialCharTok{+}
  \FunctionTok{theme}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{axis.text.x =} \FunctionTok{element\_text}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{angle =} \DecValTok{45}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{hjust =} \DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{),}
        \AttributeTok{legend.position =} \StringTok{"top"}\NormalTok{,}
        \AttributeTok{plot.title =} \FunctionTok{element\_text}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{hjust =} \FloatTok{0.5}\NormalTok{)) }\SpecialCharTok{+}
  \FunctionTok{coord\_cartesian}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{ylim =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{5}\NormalTok{))}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\pandocbounded{\includegraphics[keepaspectratio]{Dissertation_files/figure-latex/unnamed-chunk-63-1.pdf}}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# 2. Dot plot with connecting lines}
\FunctionTok{ggplot}\NormalTok{(item\_gap\_data, }\FunctionTok{aes}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{y =} \FunctionTok{reorder}\NormalTok{(item, gap))) }\SpecialCharTok{+}
  \FunctionTok{geom\_segment}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{aes}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{x =}\NormalTok{ ai\_disclosure, }\AttributeTok{xend =}\NormalTok{ ai\_use,}
                   \AttributeTok{yend =} \FunctionTok{reorder}\NormalTok{(item, gap)),}
               \AttributeTok{colour =} \StringTok{"grey70"}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{linewidth =} \DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{+}
  \FunctionTok{geom\_point}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{aes}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{x =}\NormalTok{ ai\_use, }\AttributeTok{colour =} \StringTok{"Intended AI Use"}\NormalTok{), }\AttributeTok{size =} \DecValTok{4}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{+}
  \FunctionTok{geom\_point}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{aes}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{x =}\NormalTok{ ai\_disclosure, }\AttributeTok{colour =} \StringTok{"Intended Disclosure"}\NormalTok{), }\AttributeTok{size =} \DecValTok{4}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{+}
  \FunctionTok{scale\_colour\_manual}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{values =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"Intended AI Use"} \OtherTok{=} \StringTok{"\#2196F3"}\NormalTok{,}
                                  \StringTok{"Intended Disclosure"} \OtherTok{=} \StringTok{"\#F44336"}\NormalTok{)) }\SpecialCharTok{+}
  \FunctionTok{labs}\NormalTok{(}
    \AttributeTok{title =} \StringTok{"Figure X. Intention{-}Disclosure Gap by Item"}\NormalTok{,}

    \AttributeTok{colour =} \ConstantTok{NULL}\NormalTok{,}

\NormalTok{  ) }\SpecialCharTok{+}
  \FunctionTok{theme\_minimal}\NormalTok{() }\SpecialCharTok{+}
  \FunctionTok{theme}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{legend.position =} \StringTok{"top"}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{+}
  \FunctionTok{coord\_cartesian}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{xlim =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{5}\NormalTok{))}

  \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}
    \AttributeTok{item =} \FunctionTok{case\_when}\NormalTok{(}

\NormalTok{      item }\SpecialCharTok{==} \StringTok{"writing\_parts"}     \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \StringTok{"Writing Parts"}\NormalTok{,}

\NormalTok{    ),}

  \FunctionTok{geom\_bar}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{stat =} \StringTok{"identity"}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{position =} \StringTok{"dodge"}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{width =} \FloatTok{0.6}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{+}

\subsubsection{QUAL CODING***}\label{qual-coding}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Check original column names that correspond to Q1\_4, Q1\_5, Q6}
\FunctionTok{names}\NormalTok{(df\_primary) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%} \FunctionTok{grep}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"q1\_4|q1\_5|q6"}\NormalTok{, ., }\AttributeTok{value =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{ignore.case =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\end{verbatim}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\#Coalesce open{-}ended text columns}
\NormalTok{df\_primary }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ df\_primary }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}
    \AttributeTok{ai\_prompt      =} \FunctionTok{coalesce}\NormalTok{(mpq1\_4, emq1\_4, arq1\_4),}
    \AttributeTok{ai\_declaration =} \FunctionTok{coalesce}\NormalTok{(mpq1\_5, emq1\_5, arq1\_5),}
    \AttributeTok{ai\_free\_text   =} \FunctionTok{coalesce}\NormalTok{(mpq6, emq6, arq6)}
\NormalTok{  )}

\CommentTok{\# Check coalesced columns}
\NormalTok{df\_primary }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{select}\NormalTok{(response\_id, condition, ai\_prompt, ai\_declaration, ai\_free\_text) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{filter}\NormalTok{(}\SpecialCharTok{!}\FunctionTok{is.na}\NormalTok{(ai\_prompt) }\SpecialCharTok{|} \SpecialCharTok{!}\FunctionTok{is.na}\NormalTok{(ai\_declaration) }\SpecialCharTok{|} \SpecialCharTok{!}\FunctionTok{is.na}\NormalTok{(ai\_free\_text)) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{head}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{10}\NormalTok{)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsubsection{Qualitative Coding}\label{qualitative-coding}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Qualitative coding analysis}
\CommentTok{\# Load data}
\NormalTok{qual\_df }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{read.csv}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"\textasciitilde{}/Desktop/ Qual Analysis.csv"}\NormalTok{, }
                    \AttributeTok{header =} \ConstantTok{FALSE}\NormalTok{,}
                    \AttributeTok{col.names =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"condition"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"ai\_use\_1"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"ai\_use\_2"}\NormalTok{, }
                                  \StringTok{"disclosure\_1"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"disclosure\_2"}\NormalTok{))}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Clean}
\NormalTok{qual\_df }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ qual\_df }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{filter}\NormalTok{(condition }\SpecialCharTok{\%in\%} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"AR"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"EM"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"MP"}\NormalTok{)) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{across}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{everything}\NormalTok{(), }\SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}}\FunctionTok{trimws}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{tolower}\NormalTok{(.))))}

\CommentTok{\# Check}
\FunctionTok{cat}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"Total rows:"}\NormalTok{, }\FunctionTok{nrow}\NormalTok{(qual\_df), }\StringTok{"}\SpecialCharTok{\textbackslash{}n}\StringTok{"}\NormalTok{)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Define tasks}
\NormalTok{tasks }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"brainstorming"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"literature review"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"evaluating"}\NormalTok{, }
           \StringTok{"rewriting"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"summarising"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"writing parts"}\NormalTok{)}

\CommentTok{\# Overall AI use frequency}
\NormalTok{ai\_use\_all }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(qual\_df}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{ai\_use\_1, qual\_df}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{ai\_use\_2)}
\NormalTok{ai\_use\_freq }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{table}\NormalTok{(ai\_use\_all[ai\_use\_all }\SpecialCharTok{\%in\%}\NormalTok{ tasks])}
\FunctionTok{cat}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"}\SpecialCharTok{\textbackslash{}n}\StringTok{=== AI USE FREQUENCY ===}\SpecialCharTok{\textbackslash{}n}\StringTok{"}\NormalTok{)}
\end{Highlighting}

\NormalTok{disc\_freq }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{table}\NormalTok{(disc\_all[disc\_all }\SpecialCharTok{\%in\%}\NormalTok{ tasks])}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Gap}
\FunctionTok{cat}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"}\SpecialCharTok{\textbackslash{}n}\StringTok{=== GAP (use {-} disclosure) ===}\SpecialCharTok{\textbackslash{}n}\StringTok{"}\NormalTok{)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{verbatim}
## 
## === GAP (use - disclosure) ===
\end{verbatim}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\NormalTok{gap\_df }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{data.frame}\NormalTok{(}
  \AttributeTok{task =}\NormalTok{ tasks,}
  \AttributeTok{ai\_use =} \FunctionTok{as.numeric}\NormalTok{(ai\_use\_freq[tasks]),}
  \AttributeTok{disclosure =} \FunctionTok{as.numeric}\NormalTok{(disc\_freq[tasks])}
\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}
    \AttributeTok{ai\_use =} \FunctionTok{replace\_na}\NormalTok{(ai\_use, }\DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{),}
    \AttributeTok{disclosure =} \FunctionTok{replace\_na}\NormalTok{(disclosure, }\DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{),}
    \AttributeTok{gap =}\NormalTok{ ai\_use }\SpecialCharTok{{-}}\NormalTok{ disclosure}
\NormalTok{  ) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{arrange}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{desc}\NormalTok{(gap))}
\FunctionTok{print}\NormalTok{(gap\_df)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{verbatim}
##                task ai_use disclosure gap
## 1     writing parts    125          3 122
## 2     brainstorming    163        125  38
## 3 literature review     50         41   9
## 4        evaluating     33         42  -9
## 5       summarising      6         23 -17
## 6         rewriting     11         36 -25
\end{verbatim}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\#condition analysis}
\NormalTok{tasks }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"brainstorming"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"literature review"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"evaluating"}\NormalTok{, }
           \StringTok{"rewriting"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"summarising"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"writing parts"}\NormalTok{)}

\ControlFlowTok{for}\NormalTok{ (cond }\ControlFlowTok{in} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"AR"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"EM"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"MP"}\NormalTok{)) \{}
\NormalTok{  cond\_df }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ qual\_df }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%} \FunctionTok{filter}\NormalTok{(condition }\SpecialCharTok{==}\NormalTok{ cond)}
  \FunctionTok{cat}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"}\SpecialCharTok{\textbackslash{}n}\StringTok{"}\NormalTok{, cond, }\StringTok{"— AI Use:}\SpecialCharTok{\textbackslash{}n}\StringTok{"}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{  cond\_use }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(cond\_df}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{ai\_use\_1, cond\_df}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{ai\_use\_2)}
  \FunctionTok{print}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{sort}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{table}\NormalTok{(cond\_use[cond\_use }\SpecialCharTok{\%in\%}\NormalTok{ tasks]), }\AttributeTok{decreasing =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{))}
  \FunctionTok{cat}\NormalTok{(cond, }\StringTok{"— Disclosure:}\SpecialCharTok{\textbackslash{}n}\StringTok{"}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{  cond\_disc }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(cond\_df}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{disclosure\_1, cond\_df}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{disclosure\_2)}
  \FunctionTok{print}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{sort}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{table}\NormalTok{(cond\_disc[cond\_disc }\SpecialCharTok{\%in\%}\NormalTok{ tasks]), }\AttributeTok{decreasing =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{))}
\NormalTok{\}}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}
