\subsubsection{Exploratory Analysis}\label{exploratory-analysis}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Create ai\_disclosure subscales}
\NormalTok{df\_primary }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ df\_primary }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}
    \CommentTok{\# Factor 1 {-} higher AIAS levels (substitutive)}
    \CommentTok{\# writing parts, summarising, rewriting}
    \AttributeTok{disclosure\_f1 =} \FunctionTok{rowMeans}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{select}\NormalTok{(., ai\_disclosure\_5, }
\NormalTok{                                       ai\_disclosure\_6, }
\NormalTok{                                       ai\_disclosure\_3), }\AttributeTok{na.rm =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{),}
    
    \CommentTok{\# Factor 2 {-} lower AIAS levels (assistive)}
    \CommentTok{\# brainstorming, literature review, evaluating}
    \AttributeTok{disclosure\_f2 =} \FunctionTok{rowMeans}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{select}\NormalTok{(., ai\_disclosure\_1, }
\NormalTok{                                       ai\_disclosure\_2, }
\NormalTok{                                       ai\_disclosure\_4), }\AttributeTok{na.rm =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{  )}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Regression for Factor 1 (substitutive disclosure)}
\NormalTok{model\_f1 }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{lm}\NormalTok{(disclosure\_f1 }\SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}}\NormalTok{ english\_proficiency }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ uai }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ condition,}
               \AttributeTok{data =}\NormalTok{ df\_primary)}
\FunctionTok{summary}\NormalTok{(model\_f1)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{verbatim}
## 
## Call:
## lm(formula = disclosure_f1 ~ english_proficiency + uai + condition, 
##     data = df_primary)
## 
## Residuals:
##     Min      1Q  Median      3Q     Max 
## -1.2625 -0.7997 -0.2625  0.5831  3.2099 
## 
## Coefficients:
##                      Estimate Std. Error t value Pr(>|t|)    
## (Intercept)          4.318579   1.158641   3.727 0.000254 ***
## english_proficiency -0.385787   0.247574  -1.558 0.120800    
## uai                 -0.014656   0.007394  -1.982 0.048867 *  
## conditionEM         -0.472404   0.205595  -2.298 0.022643 *  
## conditionMP         -0.147427   0.207397  -0.711 0.478032    
## ---
## Signif. codes:  0 '***' 0.001 '**' 0.01 '*' 0.05 '.' 0.1 ' ' 1
## 
## Residual standard error: 1.185 on 194 degrees of freedom
##   (2 observations deleted due to missingness)
## Multiple R-squared:  0.04496,    Adjusted R-squared:  0.02527 
## F-statistic: 2.283 on 4 and 194 DF,  p-value: 0.06183
\end{verbatim}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Regression for Factor 2 (assistive disclosure)}
\NormalTok{model\_f2 }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{lm}\NormalTok{(disclosure\_f2 }\SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}}\NormalTok{ english\_proficiency }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ uai }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ condition,}
               \AttributeTok{data =}\NormalTok{ df\_primary)}
\FunctionTok{summary}\NormalTok{(model\_f2)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{verbatim}
## 
## Call:
## lm(formula = disclosure_f2 ~ english_proficiency + uai + condition, 
##     data = df_primary)

## Residuals:
##      Min       1Q   Median       3Q      Max 
## -1.89543 -0.94501 -0.07463  0.89685  2.55791

## conditionEM         -0.327725   0.208323  -1.573  0.11731   
## conditionMP          0.125611   0.210149   0.598  0.55072   
## ---

\end{verbatim}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Gap for each subscale vs ai\_use composite}
\NormalTok{df\_primary }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ df\_primary }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}
    \AttributeTok{gap\_f1 =}\NormalTok{ ai\_use\_composite }\SpecialCharTok{{-}}\NormalTok{ disclosure\_f1,}
    \AttributeTok{gap\_f2 =}\NormalTok{ ai\_use\_composite }\SpecialCharTok{{-}}\NormalTok{ disclosure\_f2}
\NormalTok{  )}

\FunctionTok{summary}\NormalTok{(df\_primary}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{gap\_f1)}

\subsubsection{Sensitivity Analyses - Full sample (n = 210, no
exclusions)}\label{sensitivity-analyses---full-sample-n-210-no-exclusions}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\#Sensitivity Analysis 1 {-} Full sample (n = 210)}
\NormalTok{df\_full }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ df }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}
    \AttributeTok{condition =} \FunctionTok{factor}\NormalTok{(condition, }\AttributeTok{levels =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"AR"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"EM"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"MP"}\NormalTok{)),}
    \AttributeTok{ai\_use\_composite =} \FunctionTok{rowMeans}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{select}\NormalTok{(., ai\_use\_1}\SpecialCharTok{:}\NormalTok{ai\_use\_6), }\AttributeTok{na.rm =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{),}
    \AttributeTok{ai\_disclosure\_composite =} \FunctionTok{rowMeans}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{select}\NormalTok{(., ai\_disclosure\_1}\SpecialCharTok{:}\NormalTok{ai\_disclosure\_6), }\AttributeTok{na.rm =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{),}
    \AttributeTok{english\_proficiency =} \FunctionTok{case\_when}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{      b2q4 }\SpecialCharTok{==} \DecValTok{1} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \DecValTok{4}\NormalTok{,}
\NormalTok{      b2q4 }\SpecialCharTok{==} \DecValTok{2} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \DecValTok{3}\NormalTok{,}
\NormalTok{      b2q4 }\SpecialCharTok{==} \DecValTok{3} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{,}
\NormalTok{      b2q4 }\SpecialCharTok{==} \DecValTok{4} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{,}
      \ConstantTok{TRUE} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \ConstantTok{NA\_real\_}
\NormalTok{    ),}
    \AttributeTok{country\_adolescence =} \FunctionTok{case\_when}\NormalTok{(}
      \FunctionTok{grepl}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"england|uk|united kingdom|scotland|wales|britain|britian"}\NormalTok{,}
            \FunctionTok{tolower}\NormalTok{(b2q6)) }\SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \StringTok{"United Kingdom"}\NormalTok{,}
      \FunctionTok{tolower}\NormalTok{(b2q6) }\SpecialCharTok{==} \StringTok{"austria"}         \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \StringTok{"Austria"}\NormalTok{,}
      \FunctionTok{tolower}\NormalTok{(b2q6) }\SpecialCharTok{==} \StringTok{"bahrain"}         \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \StringTok{"Bahrain"}\NormalTok{,}
      \FunctionTok{tolower}\NormalTok{(b2q6) }\SpecialCharTok{==} \StringTok{"bangladesh"}      \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \StringTok{"Bangladesh"}\NormalTok{,}
      \FunctionTok{tolower}\NormalTok{(b2q6) }\SpecialCharTok{==} \StringTok{"canada"}          \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \StringTok{"Canada"}\NormalTok{,}
      \FunctionTok{tolower}\NormalTok{(b2q6) }\SpecialCharTok{==} \StringTok{"china"}           \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \StringTok{"China"}\NormalTok{,}
      \FunctionTok{tolower}\NormalTok{(b2q6) }\SpecialCharTok{==} \StringTok{"colombia"}        \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \StringTok{"Colombia"}\NormalTok{,}
      \FunctionTok{tolower}\NormalTok{(b2q6) }\SpecialCharTok{==} \StringTok{"ecuador"}         \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \StringTok{"Ecuador"}\NormalTok{,}
      \FunctionTok{tolower}\NormalTok{(b2q6) }\SpecialCharTok{==} \StringTok{"germany"}         \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \StringTok{"Germany"}\NormalTok{,}
      \FunctionTok{tolower}\NormalTok{(b2q6) }\SpecialCharTok{==} \StringTok{"ghana"}           \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \StringTok{"Ghana"}\NormalTok{,}
      \FunctionTok{tolower}\NormalTok{(b2q6) }\SpecialCharTok{==} \StringTok{"india"}           \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \StringTok{"India"}\NormalTok{,}
      \FunctionTok{tolower}\NormalTok{(b2q6) }\SpecialCharTok{==} \StringTok{"indonesia"}       \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \StringTok{"Indonesia"}\NormalTok{,}
      \FunctionTok{tolower}\NormalTok{(b2q6) }\SpecialCharTok{==} \StringTok{"ireland"}         \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \StringTok{"Ireland"}\NormalTok{,}
      \FunctionTok{tolower}\NormalTok{(b2q6) }\SpecialCharTok{==} \StringTok{"italy"}           \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \StringTok{"Italy"}\NormalTok{,}
      \FunctionTok{tolower}\NormalTok{(b2q6) }\SpecialCharTok{==} \StringTok{"jamaica"}         \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \StringTok{"Jamaica"}\NormalTok{,}

      \FunctionTok{tolower}\NormalTok{(b2q6) }\SpecialCharTok{==} \StringTok{"latvia"}          \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \StringTok{"Latvia"}\NormalTok{,}
      \FunctionTok{tolower}\NormalTok{(b2q6) }\SpecialCharTok{==} \StringTok{"malaysia"}        \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \StringTok{"Malaysia"}\NormalTok{,}
      \FunctionTok{tolower}\NormalTok{(b2q6) }\SpecialCharTok{==} \StringTok{"nepal"}           \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \StringTok{"Nepal"}\NormalTok{,}
      \FunctionTok{tolower}\NormalTok{(b2q6) }\SpecialCharTok{==} \StringTok{"nigeria"}         \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \StringTok{"Nigeria"}\NormalTok{,}

      \FunctionTok{tolower}\NormalTok{(b2q6) }\SpecialCharTok{==} \StringTok{"peru"}            \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \StringTok{"Peru"}\NormalTok{,}
      \FunctionTok{tolower}\NormalTok{(b2q6) }\SpecialCharTok{==} \StringTok{"romania"}         \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \StringTok{"Romania"}\NormalTok{,}
      \FunctionTok{tolower}\NormalTok{(b2q6) }\SpecialCharTok{==} \StringTok{"saudi arabia"}    \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \StringTok{"Saudi Arabia"}\NormalTok{,}
      \FunctionTok{tolower}\NormalTok{(b2q6) }\SpecialCharTok{==} \StringTok{"serbia"}          \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \StringTok{"Serbia"}\NormalTok{,}
      \FunctionTok{tolower}\NormalTok{(b2q6) }\SpecialCharTok{==} \StringTok{"south africa"}    \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \StringTok{"South Africa"}\NormalTok{,}
      \FunctionTok{tolower}\NormalTok{(b2q6) }\SpecialCharTok{==} \StringTok{"south korea"}     \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \StringTok{"South Korea"}\NormalTok{,}
      \FunctionTok{tolower}\NormalTok{(b2q6) }\SpecialCharTok{==} \StringTok{"spain"}           \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \StringTok{"Spain"}\NormalTok{,}
      \FunctionTok{grepl}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"united arab emirates|uae"}\NormalTok{, }\FunctionTok{tolower}\NormalTok{(b2q6)) }\SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \StringTok{"United Arab Emirates"}\NormalTok{,}
      \FunctionTok{tolower}\NormalTok{(b2q6) }\SpecialCharTok{==} \StringTok{"united states"} \SpecialCharTok{|} \FunctionTok{tolower}\NormalTok{(b2q6) }\SpecialCharTok{==} \StringTok{"usa"} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \StringTok{"United States"}\NormalTok{,}
      \FunctionTok{tolower}\NormalTok{(b2q6) }\SpecialCharTok{==} \StringTok{"zimbabwe"}        \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \StringTok{"Zimbabwe"}\NormalTok{,}
      \FunctionTok{tolower}\NormalTok{(b2q6) }\SpecialCharTok{==} \StringTok{"argentina"}       \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \StringTok{"Argentina"}\NormalTok{,}

      \ConstantTok{TRUE} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \ConstantTok{NA\_character\_}
\NormalTok{    ),}
    \AttributeTok{uai =}\NormalTok{ uai\_scores[country\_adolescence]}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Regression + bootstrapped CIs}
\NormalTok{sa1\_model }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{lm}\NormalTok{(ai\_disclosure\_composite }\SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}}\NormalTok{ english\_proficiency }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ uai }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ condition,}
                \AttributeTok{data =}\NormalTok{ df\_full)}
\FunctionTok{summary}\NormalTok{(sa1\_model)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{verbatim}
## 
## Call:
## lm(formula = ai_disclosure_composite ~ english_proficiency + 
##     uai + condition, data = df_full)
## 
## Residuals:
##     Min      1Q  Median      3Q     Max 
## -1.5239 -0.7699 -0.1891  0.6442  2.8968 
## 
## Coefficients:
##                      Estimate Std. Error t value Pr(>|t|)    
## (Intercept)          3.775957   0.992379   3.805 0.000188 ***
## english_proficiency -0.217575   0.211818  -1.027 0.305559    
## uai                 -0.010906   0.006146  -1.774 0.077504 .  
## conditionEM         -0.420744   0.177278  -2.373 0.018560 *  
## conditionMP         -0.001536   0.177979  -0.009 0.993121    
## ---
## Signif. codes:  0 '***' 0.001 '**' 0.01 '*' 0.05 '.' 0.1 ' ' 1
## 
## Residual standard error: 1.041 on 203 degrees of freedom
##   (2 observations deleted due to missingness)
## Multiple R-squared:  0.04579,    Adjusted R-squared:  0.02698 
## F-statistic: 2.435 on 4 and 203 DF,  p-value: 0.04851
\end{verbatim}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\NormalTok{boot\_sa1 }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \ControlFlowTok{function}\NormalTok{(data, indices) \{}
\NormalTok{  d }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ data[indices, ]}
\NormalTok{  fit }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{lm}\NormalTok{(ai\_disclosure\_composite }\SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}}\NormalTok{ english\_proficiency }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ uai }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ condition, }\AttributeTok{data =}\NormalTok{ d)}
  \FunctionTok{return}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{coef}\NormalTok{(fit))}
\NormalTok{\}}

\FunctionTok{set.seed}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{123}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{boot\_sa1\_results }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{boot}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{data =}\NormalTok{ df\_full, }\AttributeTok{statistic =}\NormalTok{ boot\_sa1, }\AttributeTok{R =} \DecValTok{5000}\NormalTok{)}
\FunctionTok{boot.ci}\NormalTok{(boot\_sa1\_results, }\AttributeTok{type =} \StringTok{"perc"}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{index =} \DecValTok{4}\NormalTok{) }\CommentTok{\# EM vs AR}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{verbatim}

## Intervals : 
## Level     Percentile     
## 95%   (-0.7621, -0.0458 )  
## Calculations and Intervals on Original Scale
\end{verbatim}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]

\subsubsection{Sensitivity Analysis 3 - manipulation check
exclusion}\label{sensitivity-analysis-3---manipulation-check-exclusion}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Excludes participants who scored below 3 on their condition{-}matched item}

\NormalTok{df\_sa3 }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ df\_primary }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}
    \AttributeTok{mancheck\_fail =} \FunctionTok{case\_when}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{      condition }\SpecialCharTok{==} \StringTok{"AR"} \SpecialCharTok{\&}\NormalTok{ (mancheck1\_1 }\SpecialCharTok{\textless{}} \DecValTok{3} \SpecialCharTok{|}\NormalTok{ mancheck2\_2 }\SpecialCharTok{\textless{}} \DecValTok{3}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{,}
\NormalTok{      condition }\SpecialCharTok{==} \StringTok{"EM"} \SpecialCharTok{\&}\NormalTok{ (mancheck1\_2 }\SpecialCharTok{\textless{}} \DecValTok{3} \SpecialCharTok{|}\NormalTok{ mancheck2\_1 }\SpecialCharTok{\textless{}} \DecValTok{3}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{,}
\NormalTok{      condition }\SpecialCharTok{==} \StringTok{"MP"} \SpecialCharTok{\&}\NormalTok{ (mancheck1\_3 }\SpecialCharTok{\textless{}} \DecValTok{3} \SpecialCharTok{|}\NormalTok{ mancheck2\_3 }\SpecialCharTok{\textless{}} \DecValTok{3}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{,}
      \ConstantTok{TRUE} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \ConstantTok{FALSE}
\NormalTok{    )}
\NormalTok{  ) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{filter}\NormalTok{(mancheck\_fail }\SpecialCharTok{==} \ConstantTok{FALSE}\NormalTok{)}

\FunctionTok{cat}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"Sample size after manipulation check exclusions:"}\NormalTok{, }\FunctionTok{nrow}\NormalTok{(df\_sa3), }\StringTok{"}\SpecialCharTok{\textbackslash{}n}\StringTok{"}\NormalTok{)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\NormalTok{  )}

\subsection{RESULTS: English proficiency: 95\% CI {[}-0.700, 0.222{]}
--- crosses zero,
non-significant}\label{results-english-proficiency-95-ci--0.700-0.222-crosses-zero-non-significant}

\subsection{UAI: 95\% CI {[}-0.022, 0.002{]} --- crosses zero,
non-significant}\label{uai-95-ci--0.022-0.002-crosses-zero-non-significant}

\subsection{EM vs AR: 95\% CI {[}-0.644, 0.014{]} --- just barely
crosses zero, confirming the marginal result (p =
.063)}\label{em-vs-ar-95-ci--0.644-0.014-just-barely-crosses-zero-confirming-the-marginal-result-p-.063}

\subsection{MP vs AR: 95\% CI {[}-0.195, 0.508{]} --- crosses zero,
non-significant}\label{mp-vs-ar-95-ci--0.195-0.508-crosses-zero-non-significant}

\subsection{RESULTS - English proficiency: 95\% CI {[}-0.700, 0.222{]}
--- crosses zero,
non-significant}\label{results---english-proficiency-95-ci--0.700-0.222-crosses-zero-non-significant}

\subsection{UAI: 95\% CI {[}-0.022, 0.002{]} --- crosses zero,
non-significant}\label{uai-95-ci--0.022-0.002-crosses-zero-non-significant-1}

\subsection{AR vs EM: 95\% CI {[}-0.014, 0.644{]} --- just barely
crosses zero, confirming marginal
result}\label{ar-vs-em-95-ci--0.014-0.644-just-barely-crosses-zero-confirming-marginal-result}

\subsection{MP vs EM: 95\% CI {[}0.137, 0.815{]} --- does not cross
zero, confirms MP significantly higher than EM on
disclosure}\label{mp-vs-em-95-ci-0.137-0.815-does-not-cross-zero-confirms-mp-significantly-higher-than-em-on-disclosure}

\subsection{MP discloses significantly more than EM, and this holds
under bootstrapping. AR vs EM remains
marginal.}\label{mp-discloses-significantly-more-than-em-and-this-holds-under-bootstrapping.-ar-vs-em-remains-marginal.}

\subsection{RESULTS: Composite gap --- mean 0.81, median 0.67, range
-2.67 to 3.83. Participants intend to use AI more than they disclose on
average.}\label{results-composite-gap-mean-0.81-median-0.67-range--2.67-to-3.83.-participants-intend-to-use-ai-more-than-they-disclose-on-average.}

\subsection{Evaluating writing: 1.12 --- largest gap, Brainstorming:
0.95, Summarising without reading: 0.86, Literature review: 0.8,
Rewriting: 0.73, Writing parts: 0.34 --- smallest
gap}\label{evaluating-writing-1.12-largest-gap-brainstorming-0.95-summarising-without-reading-0.86-literature-review-0.8-rewriting-0.73-writing-parts-0.34-smallest-gap}

\subsubsection{CondiCondition-level ANOVA on disclosure
gap}\label{condicondition-level-anova-on-disclosure-gap}

\subsection{RESULTS: F(2, 199) = 2.546, p = .081. p = .081 means the
disclosure gap does not significantly differ across the three conditions
at α =
.05.}\label{results-f2-199-2.546-p-.081.-p-.081-means-the-disclosure-gap-does-not-significantly-differ-across-the-three-conditions-at-ux3b1-.05.}

\subsection{There is a disclosure gap across all participants (mean =
0.81) --- people intend to use AI more than they disclose. The gap is
descriptively larger in EM than MP. But the gap does not significantly
differ across conditions (F(2, 199) = 2.55, p =
.081)}\label{there-is-a-disclosure-gap-across-all-participants-mean-0.81-people-intend-to-use-ai-more-than-they-disclose.-the-gap-is-descriptively-larger-in-em-than-mp.-but-the-gap-does-not-significantly-differ-across-conditions-f2-199-2.55-p-.081}

\end{document}
